Pacific Cannabis & Hemp Business Directory — AUDITED (Categories 1–12)

Last audited: 1 November 2026. States: Oregon, Washington, Alaska and Hawaii. California is covered separately and is excluded here except for a California-based business with a verified office in one of these four states. Cut-off 1 November 2026 — no 3 November 2026 election result is included or guessed. This file is an independent audit of the attached complete directory (196 entries, dated 2026-11-01). It was not written by the original author. The original file is preserved separately unchanged; this audited file supersedes it for counting.

Read this first — what this audit does and does not prove. Every entry was checked against its own website first, then against registry/licence lookups as far as those sources could actually be opened on 1 November 2026. State business registries (Oregon SOS, Washington SOS Corporations & Charities, Alaska DCCED, Hawaii DCCA) and bar, CPA, contractor, security, insurance-producer and hemp licence lookups are largely form-gated and were NOT directly opened in this audit pass, and a browser rate limit (429) interrupted some own-site re-opens. Those re-opens are marked as not completed, not as site failures. Therefore no bar, CPA, contractor, security, insurance or hemp licence/registration number in this file is marked Confirmed on a regulator lookup alone — numbers shown are as published on the business's own site or in a listing, and are labelled Verify unless the audit row says otherwise. The full OLCC / Washington LCB / Alaska AMCO / Hawaii dispensary-and-lab name-by-name cross-check (business + owners) was NOT completed and this file is NOT a clearance on that point. An Unverified verdict in this audit usually means “could not be re-confirmed on a direct open in this pass”, not “proved closed”. Do not present Unverified entries as confirmed, and do not present them as closed without the follow-up check named in the audit row.

Strict-reading corrections to the original cover notes. (1) Categories 5 + 6 combined: on a strict named-client test the original said 35 not 38 if Oatridge, Arux and Allied were dropped. This audit passes Arux narrowly (dedicated own-site “Cannabis Operations” section, WA-only) and fails Oatridge (generic blog mention only) and Allied Universal Hawaii (no Hawaii cannabis client found) — so the strict combined figure is 36, not 35, if only Oatridge and Allied-Hawaii are dropped, and 37 if only the confirmed-count question is about Hawaii. Do not quote 35 without this update. (2) Category 12: the original said 31 not 32 if CBD of Spokane is a plain reseller. This audit does NOT prove CBD of Spokane is a plain reseller — it finds a Yelp place record listing it permanently closed, conflicting addresses/phones, and stale own product pages — so it is kept as Unverified, not removed, and the Category 12 kept total stays 32 pending a live recheck. (3) Category 8, Oregon: Rexius is kept as Unverified for current cannabis acceptance (single July 2018 BioCycle citation only); if current acceptance is required, Oregon Category 8 becomes zero. (4) Category 7, DPS Armored: Denver-address conflict unresolved, no verified WA street office — kept as Unverified, not as a street-verified WA-office firm. (5) Category 12 licence numbers: still ZERO verified — no firm-specific hemp licence/registration number was verified for any Category 12 maker in this audit. Endorsement dates, USDA-organic claims, lab numbers and trademark numbers are NOT licence numbers.

Same-name and place traps applied. Portland OR (not ME); Vancouver WA (not BC) for ALK Global, Arux, Capacity, Navigate and others; Salem OR; Springfield OR; Bellevue WA (not NE) for Cedar Grove and Kelly Right; Everett WA; Aberdeen WA; Palmer AK (not MA) for Mado CBD (Palmer location Verify, do not assign without checking listing structure); Kailua-Kona on Hawaiʻi Island (not Kailua on Oʻahu) for Pacific States Legal Group and Mana Artisan Botanics; Waimea on Kauaʻi vs Waimea on Hawaiʻi Island (Cades Schutte Waimea office island Verify; Hemp Hale Waimea is a grant-serving area, not an HQ). No Canadian business is listed; cross-border service (e.g. R&R Heating serving Coeur d'Alene, ID; SSNW serving Alaska from a WA HQ) is recorded as a fact about a US business, not as a second office.

Duplicates and parents counted once. Vicente = Emerge successor; Ballard Spahr = Lane Powell successor (1 Jan 2025); Baker Tilly = Moss Adams successor (3 June 2025); Ashurst Perkins Coie = Perkins Coie successor (June 2026); GreenForce Staffing parent Vangst (acquired Sept 2024); The Caputo Group = The HR Group / WedoHR; GrowGeneration counted once (Tigard/Tacoma/Bellingham; Eugene store Permanently Closed; Aqua Serene = predecessor only); Elevated Equipment Supply = successor to GreenCoast Portland Commercial Services Center; Eugene Garden & Harvest Center = Groop; Spokane Organics LLC = DBA Spokane Organic and Hydroponic Supply; Honolulu Hydroponics LLC / Aloha Mushroom Company = one firm; Northern Light vs Northern Lights = same firm; Safe and Sound Security (Portland) vs Safe & Sound Alarm Systems (Bend) = CONFIRMED DISTINCT, do not merge; Canna-Pet vs Canna Companion = DISTINCT, do not merge; Birch Alaskan Naturals vs Birch Alaska LLC / Canopy = DISTINCT, do not merge; Pono Security = tradename of DWR Security LLC (GDA-29081, Hawaii DCCA minutes 17 Nov 2021), NOT Pono Life Maui / Pono Life Sciences (licensed dispensary), do not merge; WM general collection ≠ WM Healthcare Solutions (formerly Stericycle); Republic Services Seattle ≠ US Ecology Alaska; Good Dawg Gravy = Crafted Better Days = Companions Best Diet Inc. Licensed operators moved out

None confirmed and moved out with a licence number in this audit. No business in entries 1–196 was found, in the sources actually checked, to hold an OLCC, Washington LCB, Alaska AMCO, Hawaii dispensary/lab or tribal cannabis licence in its own name. This is NOT a clearance. The full name-by-name cross-check against the OLCC, LCB and AMCO licensee lists (business + named owners) was not performed — those lists are form-gated and were not opened in this pass — and must still be run before this directory is presented as licence-free. In particular, brokers who broker other businesses' licences are NOT licensees (Optic, Green Rush, Underwoods, Nichols/Souder, Kelly Right, Colliers as exclusive advisor — the licences in their listings belong to sellers/operators, including the unnamed Puget Sound Tier 3 operator in the Colliers offering, licence 02014924 in the offering, under NDA), and counsel who represent licensees are NOT licensees (e.g. Birch Horton counsel to Great Northern Cannabis Inc. retail licence #11966, AMCO Case AB18-0251; JDW Counsel counsel to SupHerb licences 23652/23653 and Sensimilla Case 2026-0055; DeWeese co-founded Green Light Law Group, a law firm, in 2019). Representation and service to licensees alone are not a licence.

Part 2 — Rewritten report (192 kept entries)

Scope (as audited): local and regional businesses based in, or actively serving cannabis or hemp clients in, Oregon, Washington, Alaska and Hawaii. Oregon, Washington and Alaska have adult-use markets; Hawaii has a medical market only. Businesses serving only hemp clients count. Each entry records its Standard category (exactly one of: Legal; Accounting and Tax; Insurance; Real Estate; Construction and Facilities; Security; Cash Management; Waste Services; Staffing and Recruiting; Compliance and Consulting; Marketing and Advertising; Software; Packaging and Labelling; Grow Supplies and Equipment; Extraction and Lab Equipment; Consumer Brands; Hemp and CBD Products), its Local/Regional/National tag, and whether it serves cannabis, hemp, both, or is a general business serving cannabis/hemp clients. No counted entry rests on a California office alone. No Canadian business is listed.

Exclusion rules (as audited): state-licensed cannabis operators are excluded (OLCC licensees and OMMP registered sites; Washington LCB licensees including social equity licensees; Alaska AMCO licensees; Hawaii licensed dispensaries and certified labs; tribal cannabis enterprises including Washington tribal compact businesses; multi-state operators headquartered in the region) — none was confirmed and moved out in this audit, but the full name cross-check is outstanding (see Licensed operators moved out). Hemp licence-only intermediates are not counted as makers (see Hemp licence only list). National companies are included only with a verified local office in the region and are marked National. Merged firms are counted once under their current name. Multi-state firms are listed once under their primary state, with all states served noted.

How to read Verify flags in the rewrite: Verify means the fact was not found in a published business source checked on 2026-11-01 or in this audit pass — it was not guessed. Where two sources conflict, both values are shown and flagged Verify. Licence numbers quoted are only those published in the sources checked; all other licence/bar/CPA/contractor/security numbers are Verify. Missing fields read “Verify”, never an invented value. Unverified entries are kept, but are clearly flagged in their row.

Research notes — audit method and limits (replaces the original interim notes for audit purposes)

Own websites were opened first for every entry in both runs, Categories 1–12, as far as the browser service allowed on 1 November 2026. Where a direct open failed on a rate limit (429), a bot-check page, a DNS/resolve failure or an HTTP 403, the audit row says so and does not claim a fresh open. State registries and licence lookups were attempted as far as searchable; most are form-gated and were not directly opened, so no registry or licence status is claimed as confirmed in this file. Search result snippets, cached summaries and directory listings were never accepted alone as confirmation of current operation — where an entry rests on those sources, it is marked Unverified. Cross-directory Already-listed checks were performed for the entries where a positive match was found (HUB, OG, LONG); other slices found no positive match in the Mountain, California or National audited directories on the names grepped, which is not a completed cross-directory clearance for every name. Same-name businesses and places were checked against the traps listed on the cover. No personal phone numbers or emails of individuals are reproduced; published work contacts for named owners and key people are retained where the business publishes them, except where the audit row says do not publish (e.g. the Canna Companion USPTO signatory email; the RMMC placeholder phone).

Cut-off 1 November 2026. No 3 November 2026 election result is included or guessed.
